# Gap table: squirrel

Made by `tools/views/gaps.py`. The 1200 species most recorded worldwide that borrow this view cover 100.0% of its 926,361 research-grade records; the 13 most recorded in Alachua County cover 100.0% of the county's 1,187. Counts are iNaturalist research-grade records through 2024. The view's own measured animal is not excluded: it shows as `m` where it was measured.

Row grades: `m` every trait of the row measured in that species; `g` every trait measured or carried by a rule; `i` at least one trait neither. The detail columns give the state of each trait.

Rows and their traits:

- **Colour**: receptor_classes, lambda_max_nm
- **Speed**: cff_hz
- **Field of view and sharpness**: fov_h_deg, binocular_deg, acuity_cpd, fovea
